## `src/ui/screens/ProfileSelect.tsx`

```
/** Who's racing? Pick a profile, create one (name, badge, experience preset) or delete one. */
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { App } from '../../app/App';
import { BADGE_COUNT, PROFILE_NAME_MAX, cleanName, type Profile, type ProfilePreset } from '../../save/schema';
import { BADGE_NAMES, Badge } from '../badges';
import { Screen } from '../components';

function ProfileCard({ p, onPick, onDelete }: { p: Profile; onPick: () => void; onDelete: () => void }) {
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const assisted = p.assists.autoAccelerate || p.assists.steering > 0 || p.assists.noKO;
  const best = p.records.classic?.bestLap;
  if (confirm) {
    return (
      <div class="mzu-card confirm" role="group" aria-label={`Delete ${p.name}?`}>
        <div class="mzu-card-q">
          DELETE <b>{p.name}</b>?<small>Their records and progress are removed.</small>
        </div>
        <div class="mzu-card-actions">
          <button type="button" class="mzu-chip danger" data-autofocus onClick={onDelete}>
            DELETE
          </button>
          <button type="button" class="mzu-chip" onClick={() => setConfirm(false)}>
            KEEP
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div class="mzu-card">
      <button type="button" class="mzu-card-main" onClick={onPick}>
        <Badge index={p.badge} size={56} />
        <span class="mzu-card-text">
          <span class="mzu-card-name">{p.name}</span>
          <span class="mzu-card-meta">
            {assisted ? 'ROOKIE ASSISTS' : 'NO ASSISTS'} · {p.stats.wins} WIN{p.stats.wins === 1 ? '' : 'S'}
            {best ? ` · BEST ${best.toFixed(2)}s` : ''}
          </span>
        </span>
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="mzu-card-del" aria-label={`Delete ${p.name}`} onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>
        ✕
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function CreateForm({ app, onCancel }: { app: App; onCancel: (() => void) | null }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [badge, setBadge] = useState(Math.floor(Math.random() * BADGE_COUNT));
  const [preset, setPreset] = useState<ProfilePreset | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const submit = (e?: Event) => {
    e?.preventDefault();
    if (!cleanName(name)) return setError('Type a name first.');
    if (!preset) return setError('Pick how much racing you’ve done.');
    app.createProfile(name, badge, preset);
  };
  return (
    <Screen id="create" title="NEW PILOT" kicker="WHO'S RACING?" onBack={onCancel ?? undefined}>
      <form class="mzu-create" onSubmit={submit}>
        <label class="mzu-field">
          <span class="mzu-field-label">NAME</span>
          <input
            type="text"
            data-autofocus
            maxLength={PROFILE_NAME_MAX}
            autocomplete="off"
            autocapitalize="characters"
            spellcheck={false}
            placeholder="YOUR NAME"
            value={name}
            onInput={(e) => {
              setName((e.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement).value.toUpperCase());
              setError('');
            }}
          />
        </label>
        <div class="mzu-field">
          <span class="mzu-field-label">BADGE</span>
          <div class="mzu-badges" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Badge">
            {Array.from({ length: BADGE_COUNT }, (_, i) => (
              <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={badge === i} aria-label={BADGE_NAMES[i]} class={badge === i ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setBadge(i)}>
                <Badge index={i} size={44} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="mzu-field">
          <span class="mzu-field-label">RACING EXPERIENCE</span>
          <div class="mzu-presets" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Racing experience">
            <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={preset === 'rookie'} class={`mzu-preset${preset === 'rookie' ? ' on' : ''}`} onClick={() => (setPreset('rookie'), setError(''))}>
              <span class="mzu-preset-title">I'M NEW TO RACING GAMES</span>
              <span class="mzu-preset-sub">Auto-accelerate, steering help, you can't crash out, boost from lap 1. Easy rivals.</span>
            </button>
            <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={preset === 'classic'} class={`mzu-preset${preset === 'classic' ? ' on' : ''}`} onClick={() => (setPreset('classic'), setError(''))}>
              <span class="mzu-preset-title">I'VE RACED BEFORE</span>
              <span class="mzu-preset-sub">No assists. Pilot rivals. You can change this later.</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {error ? (
          <p class="mzu-error" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <button type="submit" class="mzu-item big go">
          <span class="mzu-item-text">
            <span class="mzu-item-label">LET'S RACE</span>
          </span>
        </button>
      </form>
    </Screen>
  );
}

export function ProfileSelect({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const save = app.save.value;
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(save.profiles.length === 0);
  if (creating || save.profiles.length === 0) {
    return <CreateForm app={app} onCancel={save.profiles.length > 0 ? () => setCreating(false) : null} />;
  }
  const active = app.activeProfile.value;
  return (
    <Screen id="profiles" title="WHO'S RACING?" kicker="MACHZERO" onBack={active ? () => app.selectProfile(active.id) : undefined} backLabel="MENU">
      <div class="mzu-cards">
        {save.profiles.map((p) => (
          <ProfileCard key={p.id} p={p} onPick={() => app.selectProfile(p.id)} onDelete={() => app.deleteProfile(p.id)} />
        ))}
        {app.canAddProfile ? (
          <button type="button" class="mzu-card mzu-card-new" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>
            <span class="mzu-plus" aria-hidden="true">
              +
            </span>
            NEW PILOT
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

## `src/ui/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx`

```
/** Settings: audio, comfort, controls (key rebinding) and save data. Every change applies live. */
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { App } from '../../app/App';
import type { KeyControl } from '../../core/contracts';
import { KEY_CONTROLS } from '../../save/schema';
import type { QualitySetting } from '../../save/schema';
import { Screen, Segmented, Slider, Toggle, keyName } from '../components';

type Tab = 'audio' | 'graphics' | 'comfort' | 'controls' | 'data';
const TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: Tab; label: string }> = [
  { id: 'audio', label: 'SOUND' },
  { id: 'graphics', label: 'GRAPHICS' },
  { id: 'comfort', label: 'COMFORT' },
  { id: 'controls', label: 'CONTROLS' },
  { id: 'data', label: 'SAVES' },
];

const CONTROL_LABEL: Record<KeyControl, string> = {
  throttle: 'ACCELERATE',
  brake: 'BRAKE',
  left: 'STEER LEFT',
  right: 'STEER RIGHT',
  airLeft: 'AIR-BRAKE LEFT',
  airRight: 'AIR-BRAKE RIGHT',
  boost: 'BOOST',
};

const PAD_LAYOUT: ReadonlyArray<[string, string]> = [
  ['LEFT STICK', 'STEER'],
  ['RT / Ⓐ', 'ACCELERATE'],
  ['LT / Ⓑ', 'BRAKE'],
  ['LB  RB', 'AIR-BRAKES'],
  ['Ⓧ / Ⓨ', 'BOOST'],
  ['START', 'PAUSE'],
];

function Controls({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const s = app.settings;
  const [capture, setCapture] = useState<{ control: KeyControl; slot: number } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!capture) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (e.code === 'Escape') return setCapture(null);
      if (!e.code) return;
      if (!s.bindKey(capture.control, capture.slot, e.code)) app.toast(`${keyName(e.code)} is reserved for menus. Pick another key.`, 'warn');
      setCapture(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey, { capture: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey, { capture: true });
  }, [capture]);
  const b = s.keyBindings.value;
  return (
    <div class="mzu-section">
      <div class="mzu-binds" role="table" aria-label="Keyboard controls">
        {KEY_CONTROLS.map((c) => (
          <div class="mzu-bind" role="row">
            <span class="mzu-bind-label" role="rowheader">
              {CONTROL_LABEL[c]}
            </span>
            <span class="mzu-bind-keys" role="cell">
              {[...b[c], null].slice(0, 3).map((code, slot) => {
                const listening = capture?.control === c && capture.slot === slot;
                return (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    class={`mzu-key${code ? '' : ' add'}${listening ? ' listening' : ''}`}
                    aria-label={code ? `${CONTROL_LABEL[c]}: ${keyName(code)}. Change` : `Add a key for ${CONTROL_LABEL[c]}`}
                    onClick={() => setCapture(listening ? null : { control: c, slot })}
                  >
                    {listening ? 'PRESS A KEY…' : code ? keyName(code) : '+'}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div class="mzu-actions">
        <button type="button" class="mzu-chip" onClick={() => (s.resetKeyBindings(), app.toast('Keyboard reset to the default layout.'))}>
          RESET KEYS
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="mzu-subhead">GAMEPAD</div>
      <div class="mzu-padmap">
        {PAD_LAYOUT.map(([k, v]) => (
          <div>
            <kbd>{k}</kbd>
            <span>{v}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p class="mzu-note">ESC / P pause · M mute · R restart are fixed.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

const QUALITY_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{ value: QualitySetting; label: string }> = [
  { value: 'auto', label: 'AUTO' },
  { value: 'low', label: 'LOW' },
  { value: 'med', label: 'MED' },
  { value: 'high', label: 'HIGH' },
];
const LEVEL_NAME = { low: 'LOW', med: 'MEDIUM', high: 'HIGH' } as const;

function Graphics({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const s = app.settings;
  const auto = s.quality.value === 'auto';
  const forced = app.flags.quality !== null;
  return (
    <div class="mzu-section">
      <Segmented label="QUALITY" options={QUALITY_OPTIONS} value={s.quality.value} onChange={(v) => (s.quality.value = v)} />
      <p class="mzu-note">
        {forced
          ? `Forced to ${LEVEL_NAME[app.qualityLevel.value]} by the page address.`
          : app.benchmarking.value
            ? 'Checking what this device can do…'
            : auto
              ? `Auto picked ${LEVEL_NAME[app.qualityLevel.value]} for this device.`
              : `Using ${LEVEL_NAME[app.qualityLevel.value]}.`}{' '}
        Lower quality runs smoother on phones and older computers: less blur, fewer buildings and particles, shorter view distance.
      </p>
      {auto && !forced ? (
        <div class="mzu-actions">
          <button type="button" class="mzu-chip" disabled={app.benchmarking.value} onClick={() => app.redetectQuality()}>
            CHECK AGAIN
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function Data({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const onFile = async (e: Event) => {
    const input = e.currentTarget as HTMLInputElement;
    const file = input.files?.[0];
    input.value = '';
    if (!file) return;
    setError(app.importSave(await file.text()) ?? '');
  };
  const n = app.save.value.profiles.length;
  return (
    <div class="mzu-section">
      <p class="mzu-note">
        Saves stay in this browser ({n} profile{n === 1 ? '' : 's'}). Export a backup file to keep it safe or to move to another device.
      </p>
      <div class="mzu-actions">
        <button type="button" class="mzu-chip" onClick={() => app.exportSave()}>
          EXPORT SAVE
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="mzu-chip" onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}>
          IMPORT SAVE…
        </button>
        <input ref={fileRef} type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={onFile} tabIndex={-1} />
      </div>
      {error ? (
        <p class="mzu-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <p class="mzu-note dim">Importing replaces every profile on this device.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export function SettingsScreen({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('audio');
  const s = app.settings;
  const route = app.route.value;
  const fromPause = route.name === 'settings' && route.back === 'pause';
  return (
    <Screen id="settings" title="SETTINGS" kicker={fromPause ? 'PAUSED' : 'MACHZERO'} onBack={() => app.closeSettings()} backLabel={fromPause ? 'RACE' : 'MENU'} wide>
      <div class="mzu-tabs" role="tablist">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button type="button" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t.id} class={tab === t.id ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}>
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {tab === 'audio' ? (
        <div class="mzu-section">
          <Slider label="MASTER" value={s.masterVolume.value} onChange={(v) => (s.masterVolume.value = v)} />
          <Slider label="EFFECTS" value={s.sfxVolume.value} onChange={(v) => (s.sfxVolume.value = v)} />
          {app.features.music ? <Slider label="MUSIC" value={s.musicVolume.value} onChange={(v) => (s.musicVolume.value = v)} /> : null}
          <Toggle label="MUTE" hint="Also M on the keyboard" value={s.muted.value} onChange={(v) => (s.muted.value = v)} />
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {tab === 'graphics' ? <Graphics app={app} /> : null}
      {tab === 'comfort' ? (
        <div class="mzu-section">
          <Toggle label="REDUCED MOTION" hint="No motion blur, camera shake, colour fringing or speed lines; a calmer field of view." value={s.reducedMotion.value} onChange={(v) => (s.reducedMotion.value = v)} />
          <Toggle label="COLOUR-BLIND-SAFE HUD" hint="Blue-to-orange energy bar with a warning marker." value={s.colorBlind.value} onChange={(v) => (s.colorBlind.value = v)} />
          <Toggle label="LARGE TEXT" hint="Bigger HUD and menus." value={s.largeText.value} onChange={(v) => (s.largeText.value = v)} />
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {tab === 'controls' ? <Controls app={app} /> : null}
      {tab === 'data' ? <Data app={app} /> : null}
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

## `src/ui/screens/WorldSelect.tsx`

```
/**
 * World select (feature `worlds`): the five worlds in tour order. Built worlds race on click; the rest are
 * locked "coming soon" cards. Each card shows a mini map of its track, the gimmick and your best lap there.
 */
import type { App } from '../../app/App';
import { TRACK_DEFS } from '../../content/tracks';
import { WORLDS, type WorldDef } from '../../content/worlds';
import { formatTime } from '../../core/math';
import { Screen } from '../components';

/** Plan-view outline of a track definition as an SVG polyline in a 120 × 72 box. */
function miniMap(trackId: string): string | null {
  const def = TRACK_DEFS[trackId];
  if (!def) return null;
  const xs = def.points.map((p) => p[0]);
  const zs = def.points.map((p) => p[2]);
  const minX = Math.min(...xs);
  const minZ = Math.min(...zs);
  const w = Math.max(...xs) - minX;
  const h = Math.max(...zs) - minZ;
  const s = Math.min(112 / w, 64 / h);
  const ox = (120 - w * s) / 2;
  const oz = (72 - h * s) / 2;
  return def.points.map((p) => `${(ox + (p[0] - minX) * s).toFixed(1)},${(oz + (p[2] - minZ) * s).toFixed(1)}`).join(' ');
}

function Lock() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="mzu-world-lock" aria-hidden="true">
      <rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="10" rx="1.5" fill="currentColor" />
      <path d="M8 11 V8 a4 4 0 0 1 8 0 V11" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function WorldCard({ app, w, current }: { app: App; w: WorldDef; current: boolean }) {
  const best = app.activeProfile.value?.records[w.trackId]?.bestLap ?? null;
  const map = w.built ? miniMap(w.trackId) : null;
  const style = { '--w-from': w.ui.from, '--w-to': w.ui.to } as Record<string, string>;
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      class={`mzu-world${w.built ? '' : ' locked'}${current ? ' current' : ''}`}
      style={style}
      disabled={!w.built}
      data-autofocus={current ? '' : undefined}
      data-world={w.id}
      aria-label={w.built ? `${w.name}. ${w.gimmick}. ${best ? `Best lap ${formatTime(best)}` : 'No lap yet'}` : `${w.name}, coming soon`}
      onClick={() => app.selectWorld(w.id)}
    >
      <span class="mzu-world-num" aria-hidden="true">
        {w.order}
      </span>
      <span class="mzu-world-art" aria-hidden="true">
        {map ? (
          <svg viewBox="0 0 120 72">
            <polygon points={map} fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linejoin="round" />
          </svg>
        ) : (
          <Lock />
        )}
      </span>
      <span class="mzu-world-text">
        <span class="mzu-world-name">{w.name}</span>
        <span class="mzu-world-tag">{w.tagline}</span>
        <span class="mzu-world-chips">
          <span class="mzu-chip">{w.gimmick}</span>
          {w.built ? <span class="mzu-world-best">{best ? `BEST ${formatTime(best)}` : 'NO LAP YET'}</span> : <span class="mzu-world-best">COMING SOON</span>}
        </span>
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

export function WorldSelect({ app }: { app: App }) {
  const current = app.currentWorld?.id;
  return (
    <Screen id="worlds" title="WORLDS" kicker="PICK A WORLD · 3 LAPS · 7 RIVALS" onBack={() => (app.route.value = { name: 'menu' })} backLabel="MENU" wide>
      <div class="mzu-worlds">
        {WORLDS.map((w) => (
          <WorldCard key={w.id} app={app} w={w} current={w.id === current} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

## `src/ui/tokens.css`

```css
/* MachZero neon design tokens, shared by the in-race HUD (game/hud.css) and the menus (ui/ui.css). */
:root {
  --cyan: #19f0ff;
  --magenta: #ff2bd6;
  --violet: #8a4dff;
  --amber: #ffb319;
  --lime: #7dff3a;
  --red: #ff3344;
  --white: #f4f7ff;
  --ink: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.72);
  --night: #05030f;
  --panel-bg: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(14, 10, 40, 0.86), rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.7));
  --panel-border: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.4);
  --panel-glow: 0 0 1.4em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.16), inset 0 0 1.6em rgba(138, 77, 255, 0.14);
  --font: 'Avenir Next Condensed', 'Arial Narrow', 'Helvetica Neue', Helvetica, Arial, system-ui, sans-serif;
  /* Base unit: scales with the viewport, larger with the Large-text setting. */
  --u: clamp(10px, min(1.28vw, 2.1vh), 20px);
}
:root.mz-large-text {
  --u: clamp(12px, min(1.62vw, 2.65vh), 26px);
}
```

## `src/ui/ui.css`

```css
/* MachZero menus (Preact). Same neon language as the HUD; tokens in ui/tokens.css. */

#ui {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 10;
  pointer-events: none;
  font-family: var(--font);
  font-size: var(--u);
  line-height: 1.15;
  color: var(--white);
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}
#ui *,
#ui *::before,
#ui *::after {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}
#ui button,
#ui input {
  font: inherit;
  color: inherit;
}
#ui button {
  cursor: pointer;
}

.mzu-layer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: none;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: max(1.2em, env(safe-area-inset-top)) max(1.2em, env(safe-area-inset-right)) max(1.2em, env(safe-area-inset-bottom)) max(1.2em, env(safe-area-inset-left));
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 60%, rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.1), rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.72) 75%);
  pointer-events: auto;
}
.mzu-layer.on {
  display: flex;
}
.mzu-layer.over-race {
  background: rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.6);
  backdrop-filter: blur(3px);
}

/* ---------- screen shell ---------- */
.mzu-screen {
  position: relative;
  width: min(46em, 100%);
  max-height: 100%;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--panel-border);
  box-shadow: var(--panel-glow);
  clip-path: polygon(1.4em 0, 100% 0, 100% calc(100% - 1.4em), calc(100% - 1.4em) 100%, 0 100%, 0 1.4em);
  animation: mzu-in 0.22s ease-out;
}
.mzu-screen.wide {
  width: min(58em, 100%);
}
@keyframes mzu-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(0.8em) scale(0.985);
  }
}
.mz-reduced-motion .mzu-screen {
  animation: none;
}
.mzu-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.2em;
  padding: 1.1em 1.6em 0.9em;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.18);
}
.mzu-titles {
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-kicker {
  font-weight: 800;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 0.75em;
  letter-spacing: 0.3em;
  color: var(--magenta);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.6em rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.7);
}
.mzu-title {
  margin: 0.05em 0 0;
  font-size: 2.2em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
  color: var(--white);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.5em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.55);
}
.mzu-back {
  flex: none;
  min-height: 2.8em;
  padding: 0 1.1em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.3);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.6);
  font-weight: 800;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
}
.mzu-back > span {
  display: inline-block;
  font-size: 1.4em;
  line-height: 0;
  vertical-align: -0.08em;
}
.mzu-body {
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior: contain;
  padding: 1.2em 1.6em 1.6em;
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.4) transparent;
}

/* ---------- focus (keyboard / gamepad) ---------- */
#ui :focus {
  outline: none;
}
#ui :focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--cyan);
  outline-offset: 3px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 1.2em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.55);
}

/* ---------- main menu ---------- */
.mzu-main {
  width: min(40em, 100%);
  background: none;
  border: 0;
  box-shadow: none;
  clip-path: none;
  align-items: stretch;
  gap: 1.2em;
}
.mzu-brand {
  text-align: center;
}
.mzu-logo {
  font-size: 4.6em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  line-height: 1;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, var(--cyan) 45%, var(--violet) 55%, var(--magenta) 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 0.25em rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.55));
}
.mzu-tag {
  margin-top: 0.3em;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  letter-spacing: 0.55em;
  color: var(--cyan);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.6em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.8);
}
.mzu-menu {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.6em;
}
.mzu-hints {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 1.6em;
  font-size: 0.75em;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.2em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.55);
}
@media (hover: none) {
  .mzu-hints {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* ---------- menu items ---------- */
.mzu-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1em;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 3.6em;
  padding: 0.55em 1.4em;
  text-align: left;
  border: 1px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.38);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(14, 10, 40, 0.88), rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.62));
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 1.2em rgba(138, 77, 255, 0.12);
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
  transition:
    transform 0.12s,
    border-color 0.12s,
    background 0.12s;
}
.mzu-item > * {
  transform: skewX(10deg);
}
.mzu-item:hover,
.mzu-item:focus-visible {
  border-color: var(--cyan);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.2), rgba(138, 77, 255, 0.16));
  transform: skewX(-10deg) translateX(0.4em);
}
.mzu-item:active {
  transform: skewX(-10deg) translateX(0.4em) scale(0.985);
}
.mzu-item.big {
  min-height: 4.8em;
  border-color: var(--magenta);
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.28), rgba(138, 77, 255, 0.22) 60%, rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.7));
  box-shadow:
    0 0 1.6em rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.35),
    inset 0 0 1.4em rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.18);
}
.mzu-item.big .mzu-item-label {
  font-size: 1.9em;
}
.mzu-item.go {
  justify-content: center;
  margin-top: 0.6em;
}
.mzu-item-icon {
  flex: none;
  width: 2em;
  height: 2em;
  color: var(--cyan);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 0.3em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.7));
}
.mzu-item.big .mzu-item-icon {
  color: var(--magenta);
  width: 2.4em;
  height: 2.4em;
}
.mzu-item-icon svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
}
.mzu-item-text {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.15em;
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-item-label {
  font-size: 1.35em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}
.mzu-item-sub {
  font-size: 0.8em;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.66);
  white-space: nowrap;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
}

/* ---------- pilot chip ---------- */
.mzu-pilot {
  align-self: center;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.8em;
  padding: 0.35em 1.2em 0.35em 0.4em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.25);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.7);
  border-radius: 99em;
}
.mzu-pilot:hover {
  border-color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-pilot-text {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  text-align: left;
}
.mzu-pilot-name {
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 1.1em;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}
.mzu-pilot-sub {
  font-size: 0.7em;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-badge {
  display: block;
  flex: none;
}

/* ---------- profile cards ---------- */
.mzu-cards {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(17em, 1fr));
  gap: 0.8em;
}
.mzu-card {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  min-height: 5.2em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.35);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.65);
}
.mzu-card-main {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.9em;
  padding: 0.6em 0.8em;
  border: 0;
  background: none;
  text-align: left;
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-card-main:hover {
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.1);
}
.mzu-card-text {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.25em;
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-card-name {
  font-size: 1.4em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.mzu-card-meta {
  font-size: 0.72em;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.6);
}
.mzu-card-del {
  flex: none;
  width: 2.8em;
  border: 0;
  border-left: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.12);
  background: none;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.45);
  font-size: 1em;
}
.mzu-card-del:hover,
.mzu-card-del:focus-visible {
  color: var(--red);
  background: rgba(255, 51, 68, 0.12);
}
.mzu-card-new {
  justify-content: center;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6em;
  border-style: dashed;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-card-new:hover {
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.1);
}
.mzu-plus {
  font-size: 1.8em;
  font-style: normal;
  line-height: 1;
}
.mzu-card.confirm {
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 0.6em;
  padding: 0.7em 0.9em;
  border-color: var(--red);
}
.mzu-card-q {
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.mzu-card-q small {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 0.2em;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.75em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.6);
}
.mzu-card-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.6em;
}

/* ---------- create form ---------- */
.mzu-create {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 1.1em;
}
.mzu-field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.45em;
}
.mzu-field-label,
.mzu-subhead {
  font-size: 0.75em;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.26em;
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-subhead {
  margin-top: 1.2em;
}
.mzu-field input[type='text'] {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 2.6em;
  padding: 0.2em 0.7em;
  font-size: 1.5em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  background: rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.85);
  border: 1px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.5);
  border-radius: 0;
}
.mzu-field input[type='text']::placeholder {
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.28);
}
.mzu-badges {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(12, minmax(0, 4em));
  gap: 0.45em;
}
.mzu-badges button {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  aspect-ratio: 1;
  padding: 0.2em;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
  background: none;
  opacity: 0.6;
}
.mzu-badges button svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
}
.mzu-badges button.on {
  border-color: var(--white);
  opacity: 1;
  box-shadow: 0 0 1em rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.45);
}
.mzu-badges button:hover {
  opacity: 1;
}
.mzu-presets {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 0.7em;
}
.mzu-preset {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35em;
  padding: 0.8em 1em;
  text-align: left;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.25);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.6);
}
.mzu-preset.on {
  border-color: var(--lime);
  background: rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.1);
  box-shadow: 0 0 1em rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.25);
}
.mzu-preset-title {
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.mzu-preset.on .mzu-preset-title::before {
  content: '✓ ';
  color: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-preset-sub {
  font-size: 0.78em;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.66);
  line-height: 1.3;
}
.mzu-error {
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--amber);
  font-weight: 700;
}

/* ---------- settings ---------- */
.mzu-tabs {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.4em;
  margin-bottom: 1em;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}
.mzu-tabs button {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 2.6em;
  padding: 0 1em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.22);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.6);
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
}
.mzu-tabs button.on {
  border-color: var(--cyan);
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.18);
  color: var(--cyan);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.5em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.7);
}
.mzu-section {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5em;
}
.mzu-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1em;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 3.2em;
  padding: 0.45em 0.9em;
  text-align: left;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.14);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.55);
}
.mzu-row:hover {
  border-color: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.5);
}
.mzu-row-text {
  flex: 1;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.2em;
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-row-label {
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
}
.mzu-row-hint {
  font-size: 0.75em;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.6);
  line-height: 1.3;
}
.mzu-switch {
  flex: none;
  position: relative;
  width: 4.6em;
  height: 1.9em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.35);
  background: rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.8);
}
.mzu-switch-dot {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0.2em;
  left: 0.2em;
  width: 1.4em;
  height: 1.4em;
  background: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.55);
  transition: left 0.14s;
}
.mzu-switch-txt {
  position: absolute;
  right: 0.55em;
  top: 50%;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  font-size: 0.72em;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.55);
}
.mzu-switch.on {
  border-color: var(--lime);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.8em rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.35);
}
.mzu-switch.on .mzu-switch-dot {
  left: calc(100% - 1.6em);
  background: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-switch.on .mzu-switch-txt {
  left: 0.55em;
  right: auto;
  color: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-slider input[type='range'] {
  flex: 0 1 16em;
  min-width: 8em;
  height: 2.4em;
  margin: 0;
  background: transparent;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  appearance: none;
}
.mzu-slider input[type='range']::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 0.5em;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--cyan) var(--p), rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.18) var(--p));
}
.mzu-slider input[type='range']::-moz-range-track {
  height: 0.5em;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--cyan) var(--p), rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.18) var(--p));
}
.mzu-slider input[type='range']::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  width: 1.3em;
  height: 1.9em;
  margin-top: -0.7em;
  background: var(--white);
  border: 0;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.8em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.9);
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
}
.mzu-slider input[type='range']::-moz-range-thumb {
  width: 1.3em;
  height: 1.9em;
  background: var(--white);
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 0;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.8em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.9);
}
.mzu-slider-val {
  flex: none;
  width: 2.4em;
  text-align: right;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

/* segmented choice (e.g. quality) */
.mzu-seg-opts {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.3em;
}
.mzu-seg-opts button {
  min-width: 4.4em;
  min-height: 2.4em;
  padding: 0 0.8em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.28);
  background: rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.7);
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
}
.mzu-seg-opts button.on {
  border-color: var(--lime);
  color: var(--lime);
  background: rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.12);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.8em rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.3);
}
.mzu-chip:disabled {
  opacity: 0.45;
  cursor: default;
}

/* key bindings */
.mzu-binds {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35em;
}
.mzu-bind {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1em;
  padding: 0.3em 0.9em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.12);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.55);
}
.mzu-bind-label {
  flex: 1;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
}
.mzu-bind-keys {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.4em;
}
.mzu-key {
  min-width: 4.8em;
  min-height: 2.4em;
  padding: 0 0.7em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.4);
  border-bottom-width: 3px;
  background: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.08);
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.mzu-key.add {
  border-style: dashed;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.5);
  min-width: 2.6em;
}
.mzu-key.listening {
  border-color: var(--amber);
  color: var(--amber);
  animation: mz-flash 0.6s steps(2, jump-none) infinite;
}
.mzu-padmap {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(14em, 1fr));
  gap: 0.35em 1.2em;
  font-size: 0.85em;
}
.mzu-padmap div {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1em;
  padding: 0.2em 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.08);
}
.mzu-padmap kbd {
  font: inherit;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-actions {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.6em;
  margin-top: 0.4em;
}
.mzu-chip {
  min-height: 2.6em;
  padding: 0 1.2em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.55);
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.1);
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  transform: skewX(-10deg);
}
.mzu-chip:hover {
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.22);
}
.mzu-chip.danger {
  border-color: var(--red);
  background: rgba(255, 51, 68, 0.18);
}
.mzu-note {
  margin: 0.4em 0 0;
  font-size: 0.85em;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.75);
}
.mzu-note.dim {
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.5);
}
.mzu-lead {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.2em;
  font-weight: 700;
  line-height: 1.35;
}

/* ---------- loading & toasts ---------- */
.mzu-loading {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 1.4em;
  letter-spacing: 0.4em;
  color: var(--cyan);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.7em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.8);
}
.mzu-spinner {
  width: 2.6em;
  height: 2.6em;
  border: 3px solid rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.2);
  border-top-color: var(--cyan);
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: mzu-spin 0.8s linear infinite;
}
@keyframes mzu-spin {
  to {
    transform: rotate(360deg);
  }
}
.mzu-toasts {
  position: absolute;
  left: 50%;
  bottom: max(1.6em, env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5em;
  width: min(36em, calc(100% - 2em));
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: 2;
}
.mzu-toast {
  padding: 0.6em 1.3em;
  border: 1px solid var(--cyan);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.92);
  box-shadow: 0 0 1.2em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.3);
  font-weight: 700;
  text-align: center;
  line-height: 1.3;
  animation: mzu-in 0.2s ease-out;
}
.mzu-toast.good {
  border-color: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-toast.warn {
  border-color: var(--amber);
}

/* ---------- small / short screens (phones in landscape) ---------- */
@media (max-height: 520px) {
  .mzu-logo {
    font-size: 3em;
  }
  .mzu-main {
    gap: 0.7em;
  }
  .mzu-item {
    min-height: 2.9em;
  }
  .mzu-item.big {
    min-height: 3.6em;
  }
  .mzu-item.big .mzu-item-label {
    font-size: 1.5em;
  }
  .mzu-head {
    padding: 0.6em 1.2em 0.5em;
  }
  .mzu-title {
    font-size: 1.6em;
  }
  .mzu-body {
    padding: 0.8em 1.2em 1.1em;
  }
}
@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .mzu-badges {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(0, 4em));
  }
}
@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .mzu-seg {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  .mzu-presets {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  .mzu-bind {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
}

/* ---------- garage ---------- */
/* The garage is a dark studio: GraphicsSystem draws only the turntable, behind the transparent stage. */
.mzu-layer.bare {
  background:
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 28% 45%, rgba(138, 77, 255, 0.22), transparent 55%),
    radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 20%, rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.1), transparent 50%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(5, 3, 15, 0) 60%, rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.08));
}
.mzu-garage {
  position: relative;
  width: min(70em, 100%);
  height: min(44em, 100%);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.8em;
  animation: mzu-in 0.22s ease-out;
}
.mz-reduced-motion .mzu-garage {
  animation: none;
}
.mzu-garage-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 1.2em;
  padding: 0.7em 1.2em;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--panel-border);
  box-shadow: var(--panel-glow);
}
.mzu-garage-head .mzu-titles {
  flex: 1;
}
.mzu-credits {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4em;
  padding: 0.3em 0.9em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 179, 25, 0.6);
  background: rgba(255, 179, 25, 0.12);
  color: var(--amber);
  font-size: 1.3em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.6em rgba(255, 179, 25, 0.6);
}
.mzu-credits svg {
  width: 1.1em;
  height: 1.1em;
}
.mzu-garage-main {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.1fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 0.8em;
}
.mzu-stage-col {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.6em;
  min-height: 0;
}
.mzu-stage {
  position: relative;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 10em;
  border: 1px solid var(--panel-border);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 2em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.12);
  cursor: grab;
  touch-action: none;
}
.mzu-stage:active {
  cursor: grabbing;
}
.mzu-stage-label {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0.8em;
  top: 0.6em;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6em;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.mzu-stats {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35em;
  padding: 0.7em 0.9em;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--panel-border);
}
.mzu-stat {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 6.5em 1fr 1em;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6em;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 0.85em;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
}
.mzu-stat-track {
  position: relative;
  height: 0.7em;
  background: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.08);
  transform: skewX(-20deg);
}
.mzu-stat-fill,
.mzu-stat-ghost {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  top: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  transition: width 0.18s ease-out;
}
.mzu-stat-fill {
  background: var(--cyan);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.6em rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.6);
}
.mzu-stat-ghost {
  background: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.25);
}
.mzu-stat.up .mzu-stat-ghost,
.mzu-stat.up .mzu-stat-arrow {
  background-color: var(--lime);
  color: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-stat.up .mzu-stat-arrow,
.mzu-stat.down .mzu-stat-arrow {
  background-color: transparent;
}
.mzu-stat.down .mzu-stat-ghost {
  background: var(--red);
}
.mzu-stat.down .mzu-stat-arrow {
  color: var(--red);
}
.mz-reduced-motion .mzu-stat-fill,
.mz-reduced-motion .mzu-stat-ghost {
  transition: none;
}
.mzu-garage-panel {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: 0;
  padding: 0.9em 1em;
  background: var(--panel-bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--panel-border);
  box-shadow: var(--panel-glow);
}
.mzu-garage-panel .mzu-tabs {
  margin-bottom: 0.7em;
}
.mzu-garage-panel .mzu-tabs button {
  padding: 0 0.6em;
  font-size: 0.8em;
}
.mzu-garage-body {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-y: auto;
  overscroll-behavior: contain;
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.4) transparent;
  padding: 0.2em 0.3em 0.4em 0.1em;
}
.mzu-choices {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(13em, 1fr));
  gap: 0.5em;
  margin-top: 0.5em;
}
.mzu-choice {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 0.3em;
  min-height: 6.2em;
  padding: 0.6em 0.8em;
  text-align: left;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.18);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.6);
}
.mzu-choice:hover {
  border-color: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.6);
}
.mzu-choice.on {
  border-color: var(--cyan);
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.14);
}
.mzu-choice.locked {
  opacity: 0.7;
}
.mzu-choice-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5em;
  width: 100%;
}
.mzu-choice-name {
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 1.15em;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}
.mzu-choice-sub {
  flex: 1;
  font-size: 0.8em;
  font-weight: 600;
  line-height: 1.3;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.72);
}
.mzu-choice-state {
  font-size: 0.75em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  color: var(--amber);
}
.mzu-choice.on .mzu-choice-state {
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-choice.tier-3 .mzu-choice-name {
  color: var(--magenta);
  text-shadow: 0 0 0.5em rgba(255, 43, 214, 0.6);
}
.mzu-lock {
  width: 1.1em;
  height: 1.1em;
  margin-left: auto;
  color: var(--amber);
}
.mzu-tagpill {
  padding: 0.1em 0.5em;
  font-size: 0.65em;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  border: 1px solid currentColor;
}
.mzu-tagpill.light {
  color: var(--lime);
}
.mzu-tagpill.balanced {
  color: var(--cyan);
}
.mzu-tagpill.heavy {
  color: var(--amber);
}

/* livery editor */
.mzu-color {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.45em;
  padding: 0.6em 0.8em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.14);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.55);
}
.mzu-swatches {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.4em;
}
.mzu-swatches button {
  width: 2.3em;
  height: 2.3em;
  border: 2px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.25);
  background: var(--c);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0.6em color-mix(in srgb, var(--c) 60%, transparent);
}
.mzu-swatches button.on {
  border-color: var(--white);
  outline: 2px solid var(--white);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
.mzu-hue {
  width: 100%;
  height: 1.6em;
  margin: 0;
  appearance: none;
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  background: transparent;
}
.mzu-hue::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
  height: 0.8em;
  border-radius: 0.4em;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ff2b2b, #ffd02b, #5aff2b, #2bffd0, #2b8aff, #a62bff, #ff2bd6, #ff2b2b);
}
.mzu-hue::-moz-range-track {
  height: 0.8em;
  border-radius: 0.4em;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #ff2b2b, #ffd02b, #5aff2b, #2bffd0, #2b8aff, #a62bff, #ff2bd6, #ff2b2b);
}
.mzu-hue::-webkit-slider-thumb {
  -webkit-appearance: none;
  width: 1.6em;
  height: 1.6em;
  margin-top: -0.4em;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 3px solid var(--white);
  background: var(--c);
}
.mzu-hue::-moz-range-thumb {
  width: 1.3em;
  height: 1.3em;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 3px solid var(--white);
  background: var(--c);
}
.mzu-decals {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 0.4em;
}
.mzu-decals button {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.4em;
  min-height: 2.6em;
  padding: 0 0.5em;
  border: 1px solid rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.2);
  background: rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.6);
  font-size: 0.75em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
}
.mzu-decals button svg {
  flex: none;
  width: 2em;
  height: 2em;
}
.mzu-decals button.on {
  border-color: var(--cyan);
  color: var(--cyan);
  background: rgba(25, 240, 255, 0.14);
}
.mzu-chip.go {
  border-color: var(--lime);
  background: rgba(125, 255, 58, 0.16);
  color: var(--lime);
}

/* buy dialog */
.mzu-dialog-wrap {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  background: rgba(5, 3, 15, 0.6);
  z-index: 3;
}
.mzu-dialog {
  width: min(26em, calc(100% - 2em));
  padding: 1.2em 1.4em;
  background: rgba(10, 8, 32, 0.97);
  border: 1px solid var(--amber);
  box-shadow: 0 0 2em rgba(255, 179, 25, 0.3);
  animation: mzu-in 0.18s ease-out;
}
.mzu-dialog-title {
  margin: 0.15em 0 0.2em;
  font-size: 1.6em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
}
.mzu-dialog-price {
  margin: 0.8em 0 0.6em;
  font-size: 1.2em;
  font-weight: 900;
  color: var(--amber);
}
.mzu-dialog-price span {
  font-size: 0.75em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.6);
}

@media (max-width: 760px), (max-aspect-ratio: 1/1) {
  .mzu-garage {
    height: 100%;
  }
  .mzu-garage-main {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    grid-template-rows: minmax(9em, 0.9fr) minmax(0, 1.1fr);
  }
  .mzu-stats {
    padding: 0.4em 0.7em;
    gap: 0.2em;
    font-size: 0.8em;
  }
  .mzu-choices {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(10em, 1fr));
  }
}
@media (max-height: 520px) {
  .mzu-garage-head {
    padding: 0.4em 0.9em;
  }
  .mzu-garage-head .mzu-title {
    font-size: 1.4em;
  }
  .mzu-choice {
    min-height: 5em;
  }
}

/* ---------- world select ---------- */
.mzu-worlds {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(16em, 1fr));
  gap: 0.8em;
}
.mzu-world {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: auto 1fr;
  grid-template-rows: auto auto;
  gap: 0.5em 0.8em;
  padding: 0.8em 0.9em 0.9em;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-from) 55%, transparent);
  background:
    linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-from) 16%, transparent), color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-to) 12%, transparent)),
    rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.72);
  color: var(--white, #f4f7ff);
  text-align: left;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.mzu-world::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  height: 3px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--w-from), var(--w-to));
}
.mzu-world:hover:not(:disabled),
.mzu-world:focus-visible {
  background:
    linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-from) 30%, transparent), color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-to) 24%, transparent)),
    rgba(8, 6, 26, 0.72);
  border-color: var(--w-from);
}
.mzu-world.current .mzu-world-num {
  color: var(--w-from);
}
.mzu-world-num {
  grid-row: 1;
  grid-column: 1;
  font-size: 2.2em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  line-height: 1;
  color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-from) 60%, #ffffff);
  opacity: 0.85;
}
.mzu-world-art {
  grid-row: 1;
  grid-column: 2;
  justify-self: end;
  width: 7.5em;
  height: 4.5em;
  color: var(--w-from);
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 0.35em color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-from) 70%, transparent));
}
.mzu-world-art svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}
.mzu-world-lock {
  width: 2.2em !important;
  height: 2.2em !important;
  margin: 1.1em 0 0 auto;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.55);
}
.mzu-world-text {
  grid-row: 2;
  grid-column: 1 / span 2;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.3em;
  min-width: 0;
}
.mzu-world-name {
  font-size: 1.35em;
  font-weight: 900;
  font-style: italic;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
}
.mzu-world-tag {
  font-size: 0.78em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.7);
}
.mzu-world-chips {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5em;
  margin-top: 0.15em;
}
.mzu-chip {
  padding: 0.18em 0.5em;
  border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--w-to) 70%, transparent);
  color: var(--w-to);
  font-size: 0.68em;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
}
.mzu-world-best {
  font-size: 0.7em;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  color: rgba(244, 247, 255, 0.65);
}
.mzu-world.locked {
  filter: saturate(0.25);
  opacity: 0.6;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}
```
